2026-11-03T17:59:39.5347808Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 29 with the QKV-only tail split behind its post-greedy gate

Round 28 showed that splitting the 7988-row QKV GEMM into a 7936-row body and a 52-row tail was 0.8 to 1.6 percent faster on all twelve Prefill GPUs, while the same split slowed O by 5 to 6 percent. This round keeps only the QKV split, removes the FA3 pair order and paired norm store candidates, and reuses the pinned round 25 image a001f901 because the split is a Python runtime overlay.

The gate keeps the round 28 rules: twelve UUIDs, both orders with ten samples each, byte equality, input change and restore, 1200 endurance calls and a margin of max 2 us or 0.5 percent. It runs after the Decode greedy gate within the same 100 s reservation. Serving logs the split once per process and the job saves those lines to results/prefill-r29-dispatch-0..2.txt so adoption can be confirmed in serving.

This commit only adds an sbatch comment so the push passes the workflow path filter.
